HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_47d65e7c0341c01f687e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:2:3\",\"root_000412\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Daha sonra devenin görünmesi, bu yüzlerdeki alçalmaya çökmüş hörgüç ve tükenmiş beden çağrışımı ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B001","root_001630/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4d3dc015aa511d07c8b3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:2:1\",\"root_001630\",[\"B001\"]],[\"88:2:3\",\"root_000412\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yüz, kuşatıcı olayın insanla karşılaştığı açık ön yüzeydir; alçalması, ardından anlatılan yorgunluk ve bedensel maruz kalışın dışarıdan okunabilen sonucu olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_54992ca03ae9522fbb9e","text":"88:3-5 supported the inferred arrow from labor and exposure back to the lowered face. The less expected change came from 88:6-7: barren plant-food and persistent hunger activated `خ ش ع` B002, so the face acquired the material profile of low, depleted ground.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7a6cecd81fc57949fb97","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:2:3\",\"root_000412\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Alçalış, yüzleri çorak ve düşük araziye benzeterek sonraki işe yaramaz beslenme düzenini bedensel bir manzaraya dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_83e7b7af320cd480b437","text":"2. **Human lowering takes on the material image of depleted terrain.** **Lexical:** `خ ش ع` B002, \"low desolate ground.\" **Cue:** within the same window, the lowered face leads into an inedible plant in 88:6 and hunger that remains unfilled in 88:7. **Role:** the ground branch turns the face into a bodily landscape whose depletion corresponds to the failed food ecology. **Abduction:** I infer an analogy between facial lowering and barren ground; nothing in the packet identifies the face literally as land. **Reading change:** the ayah can be read spatially as well as affectively: the faces have become low, drained surfaces, anticipating the barren nourishment that sustains neither flesh nor need.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_86bd504c2838425addb2","text":"3. **Camel-hump depletion as bodily impoverishment.** Evidence: `خ ش ع` B004 flattened depleted hump; later `ء ب ل` B001 camels, `س م ن` B001 fatness negated in 88:7, `ج و ع` B001 hunger. Structural cue: the later camel-inspection and the hunger/fatness denial activate animal-body morphology. Role: the face’s khushūʿ can be read through depleted hump/fat imagery. Abductive move: the surah’s pastoral body lexicon lets a human face borrow camel depletion as a sign of failed nourishment. Reading change: the faces are not merely submissive; they look drained of height and stored life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_977286002554aa9fa94d","text":"The later camel question was the main surprise: it activated `خ ش ع` B004 and made the humbled faces visibly animal-bodied and depleted. The high garden and raised sky also made the low face part of a vertical contrast rather than just a moral state.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a8db9bb0829df25580d8","text":"2. **The face as terrain: lowered ground under a vertical surah.** Evidence: `خ ش ع` B002 low desolate ground; later `ج نن` B003 garden, `ع ل و` B001 height, `ر ف ع` B001 raising, `ء ر ض` B001 lower ground. Structural cue: the surah later maps high garden, raised sky, set mountains, and spread earth. Role: `خاشعة` makes faces terrain-like, flattened below the later vertical architecture. Abductive move: the packet uses bodies and landforms analogically; faces can be read as landscapes. Reading change: “faces humbled” also reads as surfaces made low, desolate, and ground-like.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000412/B001","root_001630/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a98d9c3d6f8a2aa5047b","text":"1. **Faces as fronts lowered before an approaching cover.** Evidence: `و ج ه` B001 face/front, B003 confrontation; `خ ش ع` B001 low humble submission; `غ ش و` B002 overwhelming affliction from 88:1. Structural cue: the face is the body’s exposed front directly after the cover is announced. Role: the face becomes the visible surface where the enveloping event meets the human. Abductive move: what covers everything first registers on the outward front. Reading change: the ayah reads as spatial collapse of the human front, not only emotional humiliation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001630/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f3a0c87f34e5fe2d5695","text":"1. **The face is the exposed front on which an exhausting mechanism becomes legible.** **Lexical:** `و ج ه` B001, \"face and front,\" and `خ ش ع` B001, \"low humble submission.\" **Cue:** the indefinite plural face is followed by two active-state adjectives in 88:3 and then by passive exposure to fire and drink in 88:4-5. **Role:** the face serves as the visible output surface; lowering registers what the later work, heat, forced watering, and hunger do to the subject. **Abduction:** I infer a causal arrow from the subsequent bodily process back to the facial posture; the text supplies sequence, not an explicit physiological cause. **Reading change:** `خاشعة` reads less as a free-standing moral attitude and more as a publicly visible posture produced by comprehensive subjection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
